# Atlan Application SDK — Capability Manifest

> Canonical inventory of every public symbol exposed by `application_sdk`,
> plus every typed Input/Output contract.
> Read this before reading SDK source. Generated by `.claude/skills/capability-manifest`.
>
> A heading marked **(deprecated)** is listed so existing code can be read and migrated, not as a recommendation: its **Deprecated** line names the replacement.
>
> **To check if current:** `uv run poe regen-capabilities && git diff --exit-code -- docs/agents/sdk-capabilities.md`

## Subpackage Index

| Subpackage | Purpose | Exports |
|---|---|---|
| `application_sdk.app` | Core developer abstractions — App, @task, @entrypoint, Input, Output, RetryPolicy, mcp_tool | 85 |
| `application_sdk.clients` | Connection clients (SQL, Redis, Azure) and ClientInterface ABC | 58 |
| `application_sdk.common` | Shared utilities — SQL filters, concurrency helpers, TaskStatistics, DataframeType | 239 |
| `application_sdk.constants` | Import-time configuration read from env vars once at import — paths, Dapr component names, observability and validation settings (use AppConfig for anything read after startup) | 163 |
| `application_sdk.contracts` | Typed Pydantic Input/Output base classes, payload safety, storage and type helpers | 72 |
| `application_sdk.credentials` | Credential resolvers (Atlan, OAuth, Git, agent), registry, vault spec | 88 |
| `application_sdk.dev` | Local-iteration helpers — embedded Dapr and Temporal daemons managed by the SDK, no host install needed | 11 |
| `application_sdk.discovery` | Dynamic App and Handler loading from "module.path:ClassName" strings at container startup | 5 |
| `application_sdk.errors` | Typed error hierarchy — AppError, the categorical leaves (AuthError, NotFoundError, …), FailureCategory and the FailureDetails wire envelope; each class's code ClassVar is the one error vocabulary | 85 |
| `application_sdk.execution` | Task/workflow execution — retry, heartbeat, sandbox, AppWorker, Temporal client | 69 |
| `application_sdk.handler` | HTTP handler framework — Handler ABC, DefaultHandler, preflight, auth, service factory | 127 |
| `application_sdk.infrastructure` | Protocol-based infrastructure (StateStore, SecretStore, PubSub, Bindings, CapacityPool) | 55 |
| `application_sdk.main` | Dev entry point — run_dev_combined() and AppConfig for local execution and container startup | 2 |
| `application_sdk.main_errors` | Typed error leaves raised by the main entry point — app module resolution, run mode, Dapr detection | 4 |
| `application_sdk.observability` | Logging context — ExecutionContext, CorrelationContext, request/correlation helpers | 155 |
| `application_sdk.outputs` | Output collectors and record models for Automation Engine | 45 |
| `application_sdk.pkl_version` | The Pkl toolchain pin — the one version CI renders contracts with, readable by an app's own tooling so a local render predicts the freshness gate | 1 |
| `application_sdk.server` | FastAPI server, MCP integration, middleware, health endpoint | 30 |
| `application_sdk.storage` | Object-store abstraction — factory, formats, batch, transfer, cloud bindings | 143 |
| `application_sdk.templates` | SQL metadata extractor templates and their contracts | 38 |
| `application_sdk.testing` | Test infrastructure — mocks, fixtures, hypothesis strategies, integration helpers | 517 |
| `application_sdk.tools` | Developer command-line tools — provision-credentials for local development | 1 |
| `application_sdk.transformers` | Legacy daft+YAML transformer stack (back-compat only — migrate to pyatlan record mappers; see upgrade-guide-v3.md). | 31 |
| `application_sdk.validation` | Offline artifact & asset validation — format-agnostic wrapper (ADR-0020) plus pyatlan_v9 .validate() wrappers, no network call | 95 |

## `application_sdk.constants`

Import-time configuration read from env vars once at import — paths, Dapr component names, observability and validation settings (use AppConfig for anything read after startup)

### Classes

#### `ShutdownBudget`

- **Import:** `from application_sdk.constants import ShutdownBudget`
- **Signature:** `class ShutdownBudget(grace_seconds: int, ...)`
- **Summary:** Every shutdown bound, derived from one grace period.
- **Defined in:** `application_sdk/constants.py`

### Functions

#### `derive_shutdown_budget`

- **Import:** `from application_sdk.constants import derive_shutdown_budget`
- **Signature:** `derive_shutdown_budget(grace_seconds: int, ...)`
- **Summary:** Split a pod grace period into the bounds each shutdown step gets, drain first.
- **Defined in:** `application_sdk/constants.py`

#### `load_shutdown_budget`

- **Import:** `from application_sdk.constants import load_shutdown_budget`
- **Signature:** `load_shutdown_budget() -> ShutdownBudget | None`
- **Summary:** Read ``ATLAN_SHUTDOWN_GRACE_SECONDS`` and derive the budget from it.
- **Defined in:** `application_sdk/constants.py`

#### `ApplicationMode` (deprecated)

- **Deprecated:** ApplicationMode is deprecated; use the v3 run modes 'worker', 'handler' and 'combined' (application_sdk.main.AppConfig.mode) — the v2 mode enum has no reader in the SDK. Will be removed in v4.0.0. Do not use in new code.
- **Import:** `from application_sdk.constants import ApplicationMode`

#### `needs_lock` (deprecated)

- **Deprecated:** needs_lock has no effect: create_worker registers neither the lock interceptor nor the lock activities, so a decorated task runs with no concurrency limit. Remove the decorator; it will be removed in v4.0.0. Do not use in new code.
- **Import:** `from application_sdk.execution import needs_lock`
- **Also importable from:** `application_sdk.execution.decorators`
- **Signature:** `needs_lock(max_locks: int = 5, lock_name: Optional[str] = None)`
- **Summary:** Deprecated and has no effect: ``create_worker`` registers no lock interceptor — removed in v4.0.0.
- **Defined in:** `application_sdk/execution/decorators.py`

#### `memory_fraction` (deprecated)

- **Deprecated:** memory_fraction is deprecated; call memory_usage_bytes() and memory_limit_bytes(), treat the fraction as unknown when either returns None or the limit is not positive, and otherwise divide usage by limit — it has no caller in the SDK. Will be removed in v4.0.0. Do not use in new code.

#### `JsonFileReader` (deprecated)

- **Deprecated:** JsonFileReader is deprecated and will be removed in v4.0. Migrate now: declare the upstream artifact as a FileReference field on your task's typed Input — the SDK's activity interceptor auto-materialises it to a local path before the task runs (with sha256 sidecar verification + parallel transfers), then read it directly with orjson / json. This reader coerces values through pandas (an int column with a missing key comes back as float, 3 → 3.0; the missing key as NaN); orjson returns the values in the file. Fix callers that relied on the coercion: row.get(key) for an optional key (row[key] raises KeyError), `is None` for NaN checks, and record any value formatted into a published key or name, since 3.0 becomes 3. See docs/agents/coding-standards.md. Do not use in new code.
- **Import:** `from application_sdk.storage.formats.json import JsonFileReader`
- **Signature:** `class JsonFileReader(path: str, ...)`
- **Summary:** JSON File Reader class to read data from JSON files using orjson.
- **Defined in:** `application_sdk/storage/formats/json.py`

#### `JsonFileWriter` (deprecated)

- **Deprecated:** JsonFileWriter is deprecated and will be removed in v4.0. Migrate now: use application_sdk.storage.rolling.RollingFileWriter (time-based rollover, heartbeat-friendly) or write JSON locally (orjson.dumps + open(path, 'wb')) and return a FileReference for the output directory — the Temporal activity interceptor persists it with SHA-256 sidecars and parallel transfers, no caller-side upload code needed. See the 'Replacing ParquetFileWriter / JsonFileWriter' section in docs/agents/coding-standards.md. Do not use in new code.
- **Import:** `from application_sdk.storage.formats.json import JsonFileWriter`
- **Signature:** `class JsonFileWriter(path: str, ...)`
- **Summary:** Output handler for writing data to JSON files.
- **Defined in:** `application_sdk/storage/formats/json.py`

#### `ParquetFileReader` (deprecated)

- **Deprecated:** ParquetFileReader is deprecated and will be removed in v4.0. Migrate now: declare the upstream artifact as a FileReference field on your task's typed Input — the SDK's activity interceptor auto-materialises it to a local path before the task runs (with sha256 sidecar verification + parallel transfers), then read it directly with pandas.read_parquet. See docs/agents/coding-standards.md. Do not use in new code.
- **Import:** `from application_sdk.storage.formats.parquet import ParquetFileReader`
- **Signature:** `class ParquetFileReader(path: str, ...)`
- **Summary:** Parquet File Reader class to read data from Parquet files using pyarrow and pandas.
- **Defined in:** `application_sdk/storage/formats/parquet.py`

#### `ParquetFileWriter` (deprecated)

- **Deprecated:** ParquetFileWriter is deprecated and will be removed in v4.0. Migrate now: use application_sdk.storage.rolling.RollingFileWriter (time-based rollover, heartbeat-friendly) or write parquet locally and return a FileReference for the output directory — the Temporal activity interceptor persists it with SHA-256 sidecars and parallel transfers, no caller-side upload code needed. See the 'Replacing ParquetFileWriter / JsonFileWriter' section in docs/agents/coding-standards.md. Do not use in new code.
- **Import:** `from application_sdk.storage.formats.parquet import ParquetFileWriter`
- **Signature:** `class ParquetFileWriter(path: str, ...)`
- **Summary:** Output handler for writing data to Parquet files.
- **Defined in:** `application_sdk/storage/formats/parquet.py`

#### `FORMAT_PARQUET`

- **Import:** `from application_sdk.validation import FORMAT_PARQUET`
- **Also importable from:** `application_sdk.validation.artifacts`
- **Signature:** `FORMAT_PARQUET: Final`
- **Summary:** Columnar. Checked by reading the file footer — no row is ever read.

#### `MODE_HARD`

- **Import:** `from application_sdk.validation import MODE_HARD`
- **Also importable from:** `application_sdk.validation.artifacts`
- **Signature:** `MODE_HARD: Final`
- **Summary:** A negative verdict fails the activity. Always a deliberate per-app opt-in.
- **Defined in:** `application_sdk/validation/artifacts.py`

#### `MODE_OFF`

- **Import:** `from application_sdk.validation import MODE_OFF`
- **Also importable from:** `application_sdk.validation.artifacts`
- **Signature:** `MODE_OFF: Final`
- **Summary:** The ``ATLAN_VALIDATE_ARTIFACTS`` kill switch is down — no check runs at all.
- **Defined in:** `application_sdk/validation/artifacts.py`

#### `MODE_SOFT`

- **Import:** `from application_sdk.validation import MODE_SOFT`
- **Also importable from:** `application_sdk.validation.artifacts`
- **Signature:** `MODE_SOFT: Final`
- **Summary:** A negative verdict is reported as ``would_block`` and the hand-off proceeds.

## Contracts

Strongly-typed Pydantic models for SDK methods. Contracts in `application_sdk.contracts` inherit from `Input`/`Output`; contracts in `application_sdk.handler.contracts` are direct `pydantic.BaseModel` subclasses.

### `application_sdk.contracts`

#### `ConnectionRef`

- **Import:** `from application_sdk.contracts import ConnectionRef`
- **Summary:** Typed replacement for ``connection: dict[str, Any]`` in Temporal contracts.
- **Fields:**
  - `type_name: str` `= Field(default='Connection')`
  - `attributes: ConnectionAttributes` `= Field(default_factory=ConnectionAttributes)`
  - `is_unidentifiable: bool` — Whether this names a Connection but its qualified name cannot address it.
- **Defined in:** `application_sdk/contracts/types.py`

#### `ContractMetadata`

- **Import:** `from application_sdk.contracts import ContractMetadata`
- **Summary:** Metadata about a contract for registration and discovery.
- **Fields:**
  - `name: str`
  - `version: str`
  - `cls: type`
  - `is_input: bool`
  - `schema_hash: str | None`
  - `deprecated: bool` `= False`
  - `deprecation_message: str | None`
- **Defined in:** `application_sdk/contracts/base.py`

#### `DeclaredFile`

- **Import:** `from application_sdk.contracts import DeclaredFile`
- **Summary:** One entry in a producer's declaration of what it wrote.
- **Fields:**
  - `ref: FileReference`
  - `label: str` `= ''`
- **Defined in:** `application_sdk/contracts/storage.py`

#### `DownloadInput`

- **Import:** `from application_sdk.contracts import DownloadInput`
- **Summary:** Input for ``App.download``.
- **Fields:**
  - `workflow_id: str` `= ''` — Temporal workflow ID for the current run.
  - `correlation_id: str` `= ''` — Caller-supplied correlation ID for tracing across systems.
  - `app_name: str` `= ''` — Per-entrypoint app name stamped into node args by the contract toolkit
  - `workflow_slug: str` `= ''` — AE's slug for the workflow this run belongs to (AUT-1124).
  - `storage_path: str` `= ''`
  - `local_path: str | None`
  - `ref: FileReference | None`
  - `skip_if_exists: bool` `= False`
  - `tier: StorageTier | None`
- **Defined in:** `application_sdk/contracts/storage.py`

#### `DownloadOutput`

- **Import:** `from application_sdk.contracts import DownloadOutput`
- **Summary:** Output from ``App.download``.
- **Fields:**
  - `status: OutputStatus` `= OutputStatus.SUCCESS` — Coarse-grained run outcome — see :class:`OutputStatus`. Defaults to
  - `metrics: dict[str, Any] | None` — Metrics collected by the OutputInterceptor (e.g. assets-extracted).
  - `artifacts: dict[str, Any] | None` — Artifact references collected by the OutputInterceptor.
  - `ref: FileReference` `= Field(default_factory=FileReference)`
  - `synced: bool` `= False`
  - `reason: str` `= ''`
- **Defined in:** `application_sdk/contracts/storage.py`

#### `FileReference`

- **Import:** `from application_sdk.contracts import FileReference`
- **Summary:** Reference to externally-stored data (for large payloads).

  - `storage_path: str | None`
  - `is_durable: bool` `= False`
  - `file_count: int` `= 1`
  - `tier: StorageTier` `= StorageTier.TRANSIENT`
  - `auto_materialize: bool` `= True`
  - `manifest_path: str | None`
  - `store: StoreTarget` `= StoreTarget.DEPLOYMENT`
  - `size_bytes: int | None` `= Field(default=None, ge=0)`
- **Defined in:** `application_sdk/contracts/types.py`

  - `CURRENT_STATE_PREFIX_TEMPLATE: ClassVar[str]` `= 'argo-artifacts/{connection_qn}/current-state'`
  - `output_path: str` `= ''` — SDK output path. Used to derive ``transformed_data_prefix``.
  - `output_prefix: str` `= ''` — Prefix to strip from ``output_path`` before deriving transformed prefix.
  - `transformed_data_prefix: str` `= ''` — Object-store-relative path to transformed data files.
  - `connection_qualified_name: str` `= ''` — Qualified name of the Atlan connection.
  - `publish_state_prefix: str` `= ''` — Auto-derived from ``connection_qualified_name`` if not set.
  - `staging_data_prefix: str` `= ''` — Auto-derived from ``connection_qualified_name`` if not set.
  - `current_state_prefix: str` `= ''` — Auto-derived from ``connection_qualified_name`` if not set.

#### `ContractValidationError` (deprecated)

- **Deprecated:** ContractValidationError is deprecated; use application_sdk.contracts.ContractDeclarationError — will be removed in v4.0. Do not use in new code.
- **Import:** `from application_sdk.contracts import ContractValidationError`
- **Summary:** Deprecated: use ``application_sdk.contracts.ContractDeclarationError`` — removed in v4.0.
- **Fields:**
  - `message: str`
  - `retryable: bool | None`
  - `cause: BaseException | None`
  - `app_name: str | None`
  - `run_id: str | None`
  - `suggested_action: str | None`
  - `category: ClassVar[FailureCategory]` `= FailureCategory.INVALID_INPUT`
  - `default_retryable: ClassVar[bool]` `= False`
  - `code: ClassVar[str]` `= 'INVALID_INPUT_CONTRACT_VALIDATION'`
  - `audience: ClassVar[Audience]` `= Audience.USER`
  - `effective_retryable: bool` — Per-instance retryable, falling back to class default.
  - `qualified_code: str` — ``CATEGORY.CODE`` string for log lines and human-readable surfaces.
  - `field: str | None`
  - `constraint: str | None`
  - `value_summary: str | None`
  - `error_code: ErrorCode | None` — Deprecated legacy ``AAF-…`` code: the per-instance override, else the class's.
  - `contract_type: str | None` `= contract_type`
  - `field_name: str | None` `= field_name`
  - `expected_type: str | None` `= expected_type`
  - `actual_type: str | None` `= actual_type`
- **Defined in:** `application_sdk/contracts/base.py`

  - `prepone_marker_timestamp: bool` `= True` — Whether to move the marker back by ``prepone_marker_hours``.

#### `TreeSelectionExtractionInput`

- **Import:** `from application_sdk.templates.contracts import TreeSelectionExtractionInput`
- **Summary:** ``ExtractionInput`` whose include/exclude filters are tree-widget selections.
- **Fields:**
  - `workflow_id: str` `= ''` — Temporal workflow ID for this run.
  - `correlation_id: str` `= ''` — Caller-supplied correlation ID for tracing across systems.
  - `app_name: str` `= ''` — Per-entrypoint app name stamped into node args by the contract toolkit
  - `workflow_slug: str` `= ''` — AE's slug for the workflow this run belongs to (AUT-1124).
  - `preflight_credential_refs: ClassVar[dict[str, str]]` `= {}` — Opt-in map of ``{ref_name: guid_field}`` for multi-credential apps.
  - `connection: ConnectionRef` `= Field(default_factory=ConnectionRef)` — Typed connection reference (qualified name, name, admin users, etc.).
  - `credential_guid: str` `= ''` — GUID of credentials stored in the secret store.
  - `credential_ref: CredentialRef | None` — Typed credential reference — preferred over credential_guid for new apps.
  - `extraction_method: str` `= ''` — ``"agent"`` or ``"direct"``. Empty defaults to direct.
  - `agent_json: AgentCredentialSpec | None` — Typed agent credential spec. Non-None when extraction_method is agent.
  - `output_prefix: str` `= ''` — Object store prefix for all output artifacts.
  - `output_path: str` `= ''` — Local or object store path for output files.
  - `exclude_filter: TreeSelection | str` `= Field(default='', json_schema_extra=_TREE_FILTER_FIELD_JSON_SCHEMA_EXTRA)` — Selection to exclude: an APITree-style nested dict, or a string.
  - `include_filter: TreeSelection | str` `= Field(default='', json_schema_extra=_TREE_FILTER_FIELD_JSON_SCHEMA_EXTRA)` — Selection to include: an APITree-style nested dict, or a string.
  - `temp_table_regex: Annotated[str, Field(pattern=SAFE_FILTER_PATTERN)]` `= ''` — Regex pattern for table/view names to exclude from extraction.
  - `exclude_table_regex: Annotated[str, Field(pattern=SAFE_FILTER_PATTERN)]` `= ''` — Form-key name of :attr:`temp_table_regex` (FND-2733).
  - `source_tag_prefix: str` `= ''` — Tag prefix for source-level metadata.
- **Defined in:** `application_sdk/templates/contracts/sql_metadata.py`

- **Import:** `from application_sdk.templates.contracts import WriteCurrentStateInput`
- **Summary:** Input for the write_current_state task.

  - `workflow_id: str` `= ''`
  - `correlation_id: str` `= ''` — Caller-supplied correlation ID for tracing across systems.

  - `workflow_slug: str` `= ''` — AE's slug for the workflow this run belongs to (AUT-1124).
  - `connection: ConnectionRef` `= Field(default_factory=ConnectionRef)`
  - `credential_guid: str` `= ''`
  - `credential_ref: CredentialRef | None`
  - `output_prefix: str` `= ''`
  - `output_path: str` `= ''`
  - `exclude_filter: FilterMap | str` `= Field(default='', json_schema_extra=_FILTER_FIELD_JSON_SCHEMA_EXTRA)`
  - `include_filter: FilterMap | str` `= Field(default='', json_schema_extra=_FILTER_FIELD_JSON_SCHEMA_EXTRA)`
